- **bill-review**: 법률안 검토보고서 작성팀의 팀장(조율) 스킬. 법률안 한 건(의안원문)의 상임위원회 검토보고서 초안을 4인 팀(검토설계자 bill-designer · 검수관 bill-reviewer · 자료조사관 bill-investigator · 집필가 bill-writer)과 조문 대조 검문소(article_check 스크립트) · 변환가 bill-exporter로 만든다. 공정: 요청 원장 → 검토설계 → 관문①(설계 판정) → ★쟁점 사용자 승인 → 자료조사·조문 원장(병렬) → 집필 → 조문 대조 검문소 → 관문②(초안 검수) → 한글(.hwpx) 변환 → 사람이 판정할 목록 보고. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/binary-classification-metric-computation--cb5ec8a407329b1a))
- **binary-classification-metric-computation**: Use when profiler outputs (KMA with mOTUs/Silva/genomic databases, MetaPhlAn 4, mOTUs 3, Bracken 2.8 on Kraken2) on CAMI in silico datasets must be scored per taxonomic rank against ground truth to compare binary classification performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/binary-classification-metric-computation))
- **binary-classification-metric-computation**: Use when comparing binary RNA-modification classifiers (e.g., m5C predictors on A. thaliana or M. musculus datasets) and you need side-by-side Sn, Sp, Acc, MCC, AUC tables from TP/TN/FP/FN counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binary-classification-metric-computation--0b64d1630941e690))
- **binary-classification-metric-computation**: Use when comparing differential detection methods on simulated trajectory data with known ground truth, where each method's per-gene (or per-state) calls must be scored as binary classification metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binary-classification-metric-computation--1e77289b2b2f2fb9))
- **binary-classification-metric-evaluation**: Use when a binary bitter/nonbitter classifier (e.g. BitterMasS Random Forest on positive-ion-mode EI-MS or ESI-MS/MS spectra) must be scored on held-out or literature-derived test compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/binary-classification-metric-evaluation--488dfa544c623bf6))
- **binary-classification-metric-evaluation**: Use when a CNN or ML model outputs binary class probabilities (e.g. DPPH high vs. low activity) and you need AUC, accuracy, precision, recall, and F1 with confidence from cross-validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/binary-classification-metric-evaluation--34ba295a857ff7a4))
- **binary-classification-metric-evaluation**: Use when you have a simulated or labelled scRNA-seq AnnData object with known per-cluster memberships and need to rank enrichment scoring methods by binary classification performance (accuracy, precision, recall, F1) per cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binary-classification-metric-evaluation--aa171ebc8116c392))
- **binary-classification-metric-evaluation**: Use when you need unbiased performance estimates (AUC, accuracy, balanced accuracy) for a binary classifier fitted on high-dimensional transcriptomic data via nested cross-validation with nestedcv, e.g. to compare against vignette-reported values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binary-classification-metric-evaluation--d7b88e6a8a30c80a))
- **binary-classification-metric-reporting**: Use when a binary-classification MB-PLS model (e.g., encoded outcome such as Female=1/Male=0 in alz_metadata.csv) has been fitted to multiassay LC-MS feature blocks and its predictive performance must be quantified on an independent test set or via repeated resampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/binary-classification-metric-reporting))
- **binary-classification-model-training**: Use when you have curated a labeled dataset of MS/MS spectra annotated as 'relevant' (compounds of interest obtained from reference standards) or 'other' (reference standards or non-target compounds), and you need to build a classifier to automatically distinguish these two classes on unknown. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/binary-classification-model-training) · 2 copies)
- **binary-classification-output-interpretation**: Use when you have executed a binary classifier (such as BitterPredict.m) on a set of molecules with chemical structure descriptors and need to translate the raw predictions into a structured CSV output file that maps molecule identifiers to their predicted class labels (bitter or not-bitter). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/binary-classification-output-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **binary-classification-performance-evaluation**: Use when a trained motif-count classifier (e.g., BOM/binModel on cell-type-specific enhancer BED labels) must be scored, and held-out predictions need F1, auPR, or regression correlation metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/binary-classification-performance-evaluation))
- **binary-classifier-ablation-testing**: Use when you have a trained binary molecular classifier (like BitterPredict) and want to understand which groups of chemical descriptors drive its predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/binary-classifier-ablation-testing) · 2 copies)
- **binary-classifier-accuracy-auc-evaluation**: Use when two or more binary classifiers (e.g. ccsmeth and the HK model) have been trained on the same training split and must be compared on held-out reads using accuracy and AUC metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/binary-classifier-accuracy-auc-evaluation))
- **binary-classifier-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when a trained binary classifier (e.g. EVA CNN distinguishing good vs poor EIC plots) must be evaluated on a large manually labeled dataset and overall accuracy validated against a published performance claim. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/binary-classifier-accuracy-evaluation))
- **binary-classifier-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when a binary classifier has produced per-site predictions on a labeled, held-out dataset and you must quantify performance as mean accuracy, AUC, specificity, and precision against gold-standard positives/negatives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binary-classifier-accuracy-evaluation))
- **binary-classifier-auc-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a trained fusion-read filter (e.g., HVLD retrained per dataset on artificial chimeric negatives and PCAWG positives) and comparing its AUC to competing models on held-out single-cell RNA-seq test samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binary-classifier-auc-evaluation))
- **binary-classifier-benchmarking**: Use when two or more binary classifier variants (e.g., a stacked ensemble vs. its base learners) must be compared on the same test set, especially under class imbalance where AUPRC and recall at probability thresholds matter more than accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/binary-classifier-benchmarking))
- **binary-classifier-evaluation-metrics**: Reference for scoring any binary classifier on expert Pass/Fail labels — e.g. MetaClean peak-quality predictions on an independent LC–MS test set such as ST000695. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/binary-classifier-evaluation-metrics))
- **binary-classifier-f-measure-computation**: Use when evaluating a binary classifier (e.g., peak Pass/Fail quality in untargeted LC–MS metabolomics) where both high- and low-quality classes are important and single accuracy is insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/binary-classifier-f-measure-computation))
- **binary-classifier-metric-computation**: Use when a per-read binary modification classifier (e.g. CHEUI-solo Model 1 CNN outputs on IVT set 1/set 2 9-mer signals) must be scored with accuracy, precision, recall, and AUC and checked against published values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/binary-classifier-metric-computation))
- **binary-classifier-metric-evaluation**: Use when a trained binary classifier's predictions on held-out test samples (e.g., the 92-sample plant R protein independent test) must be scored with accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and AUC and compared to published values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/binary-classifier-metric-evaluation))
- **binary-classifier-metric-evaluation**: Use when a trained binary classifier (e.g. a Keras/TensorFlow viral-read model) must be scored on a labeled held-out test partition and compared to baselines under identical data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binary-classifier-metric-evaluation))
- **binary-classifier-performance-metrics**: Use when several tuned binary classifiers (e.g. LR, DT, SVM, RF, XGBoost) must be ranked on a held-out labeled test set to decide which algorithm a tool should adopt, as in LncDC's five-algorithm selection on H-Test. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binary-classifier-performance-metrics))
- **binary-classifier-power-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when a binary classifier trained on simulated OU-process expression data (or any two-class problem) must be scored for power and accuracy on a balanced test set, especially after training on skewed/unbalanced training data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binary-classifier-power-accuracy-evaluation))
- **binary-container-vs-crypto**: 判定未知二进制载荷到底是"加密"还是"自定义/压缩格式"的判死流程，核心是每条假设都必须配零假设或阴性对照，防止把统计巧合当破口。当遇到解析器读不出的文件（UnityPy/标准格式解析出 0 对象、无 magic、熵偏高但不到 8.0、"打不开但能列出文件名"）、既有逆向小项目卡在"要不要先拿密钥"、用户问"这到底是加密还是格式"、或需要复核上一轮"已排除的假设"是否真的有对照时使用。触发词：未知二进制、是加密还是格式、熵、IC、重合指数、共密钥流、两时间垫、many-time-pad、差分签名、周期攻击、重复块、ECB、已知明文攻击、fieldDefaultValues、LuaConfDataReader、sharecfgdata、假绿灯、判死、IL2CPP 反汇编、exec-out。 ([MrKLLM/azurlane-assetbundle-toolkit](https://github.com/MrKLLM/azurlane-assetbundle-toolkit/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/binary-container-vs-crypto) · ★7)
- **binary-credential-format-boundary**: Audit and repair readers for fixed-length binary credentials and their text encodings without mutating raw bytes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/binary-cross-entropy-classification-evaluation))
- **binary-cross-entropy-loss-optimization**: Use when you have a pre-trained TCN spectrum encoder, annotated MS/MS spectra paired with ground-truth molecular formulas, and you want to train only the formula ranking and rescoring components without retraining the spectrum feature extractor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/binary-cross-entropy-loss-optimization))
- **binary-cross-entropy-loss-optimization**: Use when when you have a pretrained spectrum encoder (TCN) and need to train a formula rescoring module that ranks candidate molecular formulas against MS/MS spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/binary-cross-entropy-loss-optimization) · 2 copies)
- **binary-cross-entropy-model-evaluation**: Use when a trained pSCNN model on NMR spectra of mixtures must be scored, or when benchmarking binary compound-identification performance with ACC/TPR/FPR metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/binary-cross-entropy-model-evaluation))
- **binary-crypto-verify**: Verifies in compiled machine code that security properties survived the compiler: that secret zeroization was not removed by dead-store elimination and that constant-time logic did not regain secret-dependent branches. Use when auditing a binary, shared object, static library, or firmware image whose source claims constant-time or wiping guarantees, when a memset on a key may have been optimized away, or when confirming a fix actually landed in the shipped artifact, using Ghidra, objdump, or radare2. Not for source-level crypto review (use crypto-source-audit) and not for general binary explo… ([Quant-Off/skills](https://github.com/Quant-Off/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/binary-crypto-verify/skills/binary-crypto-verify) · ★1)
